function setMode(m){mode=m;if(drag&&action)updateAim(drag.current);}
function updateScore(){$('score').textContent=match.us+' — '+match.them;}
function updateDistance(){if(!action)return;const d=Math.hypot(action.hero.x,action.hero.z+20);$('distance').textContent=Math.round(d)+' m du but';}
function setup(defer=false){$('tacticalPitch').hidden=true;$('match').classList.toggle('simulating',false);$('matchPlayback').hidden=true;const s=match.training?scenarios[0]:match.deck[match.index%match.deck.length];match.learned=false;$('lessonPanel').hidden=!match.training;$('lessonNext').hidden=true;if(match.training)$('lessonHint').textContent=(match.index+1)+' / 6 · '+lessons[match.index][1];if(!defer)matchEvents(match.training?0:[23,57,86][match.index]);action={sim:0,started:false,start:{...s.start},hero:actor({x:s.start.x,z:s.start.z+.58},{isHero:true}),receiver:s.receiver?actor(s.receiver,{angle:Math.PI*.5}):null,defenders:(match.training?[]:s.defenders).map(p=>actor(p,{angle:Math.PI})),ball:{...s.start,y:R,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0},keeper:createKeeper(),ready:true,phase:'ready',flying:false,ended:false,after:0,flightTime:0,dribbles:0,pressIndex:0,possessionTime:0,ballControl:'loose',combination:false,shotBy:'hero'};action.teammates=action.receiver?[action.receiver]:[];if(!match.training){for(const pos of [{x:-8,z:-12.5},{x:8,z:-10},{x:1,z:-8.5}]){if(action.teammates.length>=3)break;if(Math.hypot(pos.x-action.hero.x,pos.z-action.hero.z)<2)pos.x=-pos.x;action.teammates.push(actor(pos));}action.defenders.push(actor({x:-8,z:-16},{angle:Math.PI}),actor({x:8,z:-16},{angle:Math.PI}));}if(!match.training){for(const pos of [{x:-10,z:-1},{x:1,z:3},{x:10,z:7}])action.teammates.push(actor(pos,{support:true}));for(const pos of [{x:-9,z:2},{x:4,z:5},{x:11,z:-3}])action.defenders.push(actor(pos,{angle:Math.PI,support:true}));}if(match.training)action.keeper.x=12;action.pressIndex=nearestDefender(action.hero);drag=null;engine.appearance=career;engine.setup(action);engine.resize();setMode(match.index===0?'low':'normal');$('continue').hidden=true;$('replayBtn').hidden=true;$('combination').hidden=true;$('gestureLabel').textContent='TIRE VERS TOI POUR FRAPPER · TAP POUR JOUER';setShotCurve(0);$('feedback').textContent='Prends ton temps : l’action attend ton premier geste.';$('powerText').textContent='—';$('powerBar').style.width='0%';$('minute').textContent=match.training?'ÉCOLE':[23,57,86][match.index]+'′';$('actionCount').textContent=match.training?'GESTE '+(match.index+1)+' / 6':'ACTION '+(match.index+1)+' / 3 · '+match.profile.name;$('kind').textContent=s.kind;$('prompt').textContent=s.title;$('help').textContent=match.index===0?'Touche le coéquipier bleu pour passer. Touche l’herbe pour dribbler.':'Touche un bleu pour passer. Tire vers toi depuis une zone libre pour frapper.';updateDistance();updateScore();if(!match.training&&!defer)beginIncoming();updateEnergy();}
$('play').onclick=()=>startMatch(false);
$('leave').onclick=()=>{ambience.stop();if(match&&!match.training){career.energy=match.energy;save();}match=null;action=null;drag=null;screen('home');};$('back').onclick=()=>screen('home');
$('continue').onclick=()=>{if(!action?.ended)return;const a=action,t=match.tactical;if(t){resumeTactical(t,[a.hero,...a.teammates,...a.defenders,a.keeper],a.outcomeKind,a.ball,a.newOwner?.tacticalId);match.minute=t.minute;}beginInterval();};
function finish(){if(!match)return;match.minute=90;const m=match,verdict=m.instruction?coachVerdict(m.instruction,m,performance(m).rating):null,base=performance(m).earned,special=scenarioBonus(m.scenario,m,base),earned=base+(verdict?.bonus||0)+special.xp,oldLevel=progressionLevel(developmentXP());career.energy=m.energy;career.games++;career.goals+=m.goals;career.assists+=m.assists;career.xp+=earned;if(verdict)career.trust=clamp(career.trust+verdict.trust,0,100);const rating=performance(m).rating.toFixed(1).replace('.',',');$('resultScenario').textContent=special.text;$('resultCoach').textContent=verdict?`Consigne du coach : ${verdict.text} ${verdict.done?'✓ Respectée (+35 XP)':'✗ Pas respectée'} · Confiance ${career.trust} % (${verdict.trust>=0?'+':''}${verdict.trust})`:'';career.last=`AC Rivage ${m.us} – ${m.them} ${clubs[(career.games-1)%6]} · Note ${rating} · ${m.goals} but(s), ${m.assists} passe(s) décisive(s).`;save();$('resultScore').textContent=m.us+' — '+m.them;$('resultFixture').textContent='AC Rivage / '+clubs[(career.games-1)%6];$('rating').textContent=rating;$('headline').textContent=m.success>=3?'Tu as répondu présent.':m.success?'Une carrière se construit.':'Le prochain match sera le tien.';$('resultDetail').textContent=(m.entered?'Entré à la '+m.entered+'′ · ':'')+(m.left?'Sorti à la '+m.left+'′ · ':'')+m.goals+' but(s) · '+m.assists+' passe(s) décisive(s) · '+m.passes+' passe(s) réussie(s) · '+m.dribbles+' dribble(s) · '+m.chances+' occasion(s) créée(s) · '+m.losses+' perte(s)'+(m.foulsWon?' · '+m.foulsWon+' faute(s) obtenue(s)':'')+(m.yellows||m.reds?' · cartons adverses : '+(m.yellows||0)+' jaune(s), '+(m.reds||0)+' rouge(s)':'');$('reward').textContent='+'+earned+' XP'+(progressionLevel(developmentXP())>oldLevel?' · Niveau général +1':'');$('resultEnergy').textContent='Énergie restante : '+Math.round(m.energy)+' % · récupération disponible à l’accueil.';$('resultEvents').textContent=m.events.map(e=>`${e.minute}′ · ${e.side==='us'?'AC Rivage':'Adversaire'}${e.scorer?' ('+e.scorer+')':''}`).join(' / ')||'Aucun but dans ce match.';ambience.whistle(3);ambience.stop();action=null;match=null;screen('result');}
function teammateAt(point){return (action.teammates||[action.receiver]).filter(Boolean).filter(t=>screenNear(t,point,16)).sort((a,b)=>{const pa=engine.actorScreen(a),pb=engine.actorScreen(b);return Math.hypot(pa.x-point.x,pa.y-point.y)-Math.hypot(pb.x-point.x,pb.y-point.y);})[0];}

function beginIncoming(){const a=action;a.incomingPlayer=a.teammates.find(t=>t.support)||a.teammates[0];a.incomingTarget={x:a.hero.x,z:a.hero.z-.45};a.incomingPlayer.x=clamp(a.hero.x+3,-PITCH.W+2,PITCH.W-2);a.incomingPlayer.z=a.hero.z+5;a.incomingPlayer.angle=Math.atan2(-(a.hero.x-a.incomingPlayer.x),-(a.hero.z-a.incomingPlayer.z));a.incomingPlayer.kickTimer=0;a.phase='incoming';a.ready=false;a.started=true;a.incomingTime=0;a.ball={x:a.incomingPlayer.x,y:R,z:a.incomingPlayer.z,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0};$('feedback').textContent='Ton coéquipier te sert… Observe la passe avant de jouer.';}
function beginRun(point){const a=action;if(!a||!point)return;const h=a.hero,mate=a.receiver||a.passRecipient,short=Math.hypot(point.x-h.x,point.z-h.z)<1.2;
 // Tap near your player: come short and ask for the ball in your feet. Tap further: run in behind and get it in your stride.
 if(short&&mate){const dx=mate.x-h.x,dz=mate.z-h.z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;a.runTarget={x:h.x+dx/l*1.6,z:h.z+dz/l*1.6};a.returnSpot={...a.runTarget};a.runKind='feet';}
 else{a.runTarget={x:clamp(point.x,-PITCH.W+1.5,PITCH.W-1.5),z:clamp(point.z,-17,PITCH.far-3)};const dx=a.runTarget.x-h.x,dz=a.runTarget.z-h.z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;a.returnSpot={x:clamp(a.runTarget.x+dx/l*1.4,-PITCH.W+1.5,PITCH.W-1.5),z:clamp(a.runTarget.z+dz/l*1.4,-17.5,PITCH.far-3)};a.runKind='depth';}
 if(!a.runCounted&&!match.training){a.runCounted=true;match.runs=(match.runs||0)+1;}a.combination=true;if(a.phase==='choice'){a.phase='offball';a.runTime=0;$('combination').hidden=true;}$('feedback').textContent=a.runKind==='feet'?'Appel dans les pieds demandé : ton partenaire choisit.':'Appel en profondeur demandé : ton partenaire choisit.';}
// Lot 1 · The start of the gesture decides: begun on a partner (or ending on one) it stays a pass, never a rake.
// Lot 2 · Off-ball runs: at every pause up to two partners show the run they want (a winger cutting inside, a midfielder breaking forward).
// They start onside; touching the end of a run, or any free space a partner reaches first, plays the ball into his stride.
function planRuns(a){a.runs=[];if(match.training||!a.teammates?.length||a.setPiece&&!a.released)return;const h=a.hero,line=offsideLineZ(a),space=p=>Math.min(9,...a.defenders.map(d=>Math.hypot(d.x-p.x,d.z-p.z)));
 a.runs=a.teammates.filter(t=>t.tacticalId%11>=5&&!(t.z<line&&t.z<HALFWAY)&&Math.hypot(t.x-h.x,t.z-h.z)<34).map(t=>{const wide=Math.abs(t.x)>9,point=wide?{x:t.x*.35,z:Math.max(-17,Math.min(t.z-7,line-4))}:{x:clamp(t.x+(t.x>h.x?2.5:-2.5),-PITCH.W+3,PITCH.W-3),z:Math.max(-17,Math.min(t.z-8,line-3))};return point.z<t.z-3?{mate:t,point,space:space(point),kind:wide?'diagonale':'profondeur'}:null;}).filter(r=>r&&r.space>1.6).sort((p,q)=>q.space-p.space).slice(0,2);}
// Lot 3 · Anywhere along a run arrow (or near its end) counts: the ball is then played to the end of the run.
function runAt(target){const seg=r=>{const ax=r.mate.x,az=r.mate.z,dx=r.point.x-ax,dz=r.point.z-az,l2=dx*dx+dz*dz||1,u=clamp(((target.x-ax)*dx+(target.z-az)*dz)/l2,.15,1.25);return Math.hypot(target.x-ax-u*dx,target.z-az-u*dz);};const h=action.hero;if(h&&Math.hypot(target.x-h.x,target.z-h.z)<7)return null;return (action.runs||[]).filter(r=>seg(r)<2.8).sort((p,q)=>seg(p)-seg(q))[0]||null;}
// Lot 4 · Open space only becomes a through ball far from the carrier and right next to a partner; anything else is a dribble.
function runnerFor(target){const a=action,h=a.hero,hd=Math.hypot(target.x-h.x,target.z-h.z);if(hd<12)return null;const line=offsideLineZ(a),def=Math.min(...a.defenders.map(d=>Math.hypot(d.x-target.x,d.z-target.z)))/6.2;
 return (a.teammates||[]).filter(t=>t.tacticalId%11!==0&&!(t.z<line&&t.z<HALFWAY)).map(t=>({t,d:Math.hypot(t.x-target.x,t.z-target.z),time:Math.hypot(t.x-target.x,t.z-target.z)/6.6})).filter(c=>c.time<1.4&&c.d<hd-4&&c.time<def+.4).sort((p,q)=>p.time-q.time)[0]?.t||null;}
function rakeAllowed(end){return drag&&drag.kind!=='ball'&&(drag.kind==='skill'||!drag.fromMate&&!drag.startRun&&!teammateAt(end));}
function screenNear(actor,point,radius=34){if(!actor)return false;const p=engine.actorScreen(actor);return Math.hypot(p.x-point.x,p.y-point.y)<radius;}
function updateAim(current){
 if(!drag||!action?.ready)return;drag.current=current;
 const previous=drag.points[drag.points.length-1];if(Math.hypot(current.x-previous.x,current.y-previous.y)>2)drag.points.push({...current});
 if(drag.kind==='target'&&!drag.fromMate&&!drag.startRun&&current.y-drag.start.y>=18&&Math.abs(current.x-drag.start.x)<(current.y-drag.start.y)*1.5)drag.kind='ball';
 let target;
 if(drag.kind==='ball'){
  const shot=assistedShot(drag.start,current);target=shot.target;mode='normal';drag.spin=shotCurve;drag.power=shot.power;drag.locked=false;
 }else{
  target=engine.groundAtScreen(current.x,current.y);const teammate=teammateAt(current);drag.through=false;if(teammate){action.receiver=teammate;target={x:teammate.x,z:teammate.z};}
  // Press a partner and slide into the space: a ball into his run instead of into his feet.
  else if(drag.fromMate&&target&&Math.hypot(current.x-drag.start.x,current.y-drag.start.y)>24){action.receiver=drag.fromMate;drag.through=true;}
  // Lot 2 · Touch the end of a run or open space: the ball goes into the stride of the partner who gets there first.
  else if(!drag.fromMate&&target&&!action.setPiece){const r=runAt(target),m=r?.mate||runnerFor(target);if(m){action.receiver=m;drag.through=true;if(r)target={...r.point};}}
  const kind=targetGesture(Date.now()-drag.began,teammate||drag.through);mode=kind==='pass'?'low':kind;drag.spin=0;
 }
 if(!target)return;drag.raw=target;drag.target=mode==='dribble'?dribbleTarget(action.hero,target):target;engine.preview(action.ball,drag.target,mode,drag.spin*attributes(developmentXP()).curve,mode==='normal'?drag.power:null,attributes(developmentXP()).force);
 const pull=mode==='normal'?drag.power*110:Math.hypot(current.x-drag.start.x,current.y-drag.start.y),spin=drag.spin||0;
 const far=false,weak=mode!=='dribble'&&weakFootFor(action.hero,drag.target);
 $('gestureLabel').textContent=(mode==='dribble'?(far?'POUSSÉE DE BALLE':'DRIBBLE'):drag.through?'PASSE DANS L’ESPACE':mode==='low'?'PASSE':mode==='lob'?'LOB':Math.abs(spin)>.1?'FRAPPE · EFFET '+(spin<0?'↶ GAUCHE':'DROITE ↷')+' '+Math.round(Math.abs(spin)/.4*100)+' %':'FRAPPE · SANS EFFET')+(weak?' · PIED FAIBLE':'');
 $('powerText').textContent=mode==='normal'?Math.round(clamp(pull/110*100,0,100))+' %':mode==='dribble'?'5 m maximum':mode==='lob'?'Relâche pour lober':'Relâche pour passer';
 $('powerBar').style.width=(mode==='normal'?clamp(pull/110*100,0,100):100)+'%';
 // V19 · On a ball in the air, a quick swipe upwards is a bicycle kick.
 if(action.phase==='aerial'&&!action.aerial?.ground&&drag.start.y-current.y>45&&Math.abs(current.x-drag.start.x)<drag.start.y-current.y){drag.bicycle=true;$('gestureLabel').textContent='RETOURNÉE · RELÂCHE';$('powerText').textContent='Acrobatie';}if(rakeAllowed(current)&&lateralSkill(drag.points,Date.now()-drag.began)){$('gestureLabel').textContent='RÂTEAU '+(current.x<drag.start.x?'GAUCHE':'DROITE')+' · RELÂCHE';}
}
function startGesture(e){
 if(action?.phase==='interval')return;
 if(guideOpen||!action||action.ended)return;
 if(action.phase==='press'){const p=engine.groundAtScreen(e.clientX,e.clientY);if(p){action.runTarget=p;action.ready=false;action.started=true;}e.preventDefault();return;}
 if(action.callPause){const p=engine.groundAtScreen(e.clientX,e.clientY);action.callPause=false;if(screenNear(action.hero,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY},40)||!p){action.runTarget=null;$('feedback').textContent='Tu restes à ta place.';}else beginRun(p);e.preventDefault();return;}
 if(action.phase==='incoming'&&action.incomingMode==='lob'&&action.incomingReleased){const p=engine.groundAtScreen(e.clientX,e.clientY);if(p){const h=action.hero,o=action.crossStart||(action.crossStart={x:h.x,z:h.z}),d=Math.hypot(p.x-o.x,p.z-o.z),k=d>4?4/d:1;action.runTarget={x:o.x+(p.x-o.x)*k,z:o.z+(p.z-o.z)*k};action.positioned=true;$('feedback').textContent='Tu te places pour attaquer le ballon.';}e.preventDefault();return;}
 if(action.phase==='offball'||(action.flying&&action.flightMode==='low')){beginRun(engine.groundAtScreen(e.clientX,e.clientY));e.preventDefault();return;}
 if(action.phase==='choice'){if(screenNear(action.hero,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY},48))chooseCombination(true);else{const p=engine.groundAtScreen(e.clientX,e.clientY);if(p&&p.z<-18.5)chooseCombination(false);else if(p)beginRun(p);}return;}
 if((!action.ready&&action.phase!=='dribble')||drag)return;const running=action.phase==='dribble';const ball=engine.ballScreen(),point={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};const atReceiver=teammateAt(point),onBall=!atReceiver&&Math.hypot(point.x-ball.x,point.y-ball.y)<36;
 if(!running&&!atReceiver&&action.ready&&action.phase!=='aerial'&&!action.setPiece&&screenNear(action.hero,point,30)&&Math.hypot(point.x-ball.x,point.y-ball.y)>22){beginShield();e.preventDefault();return;}
 canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);drag={id:e.pointerId,kind:running?'skill':onBall?'ball':'target',start:point,current:point,points:[point],spin:0,target:null,began:Date.now(),fromMate:atReceiver||null};if(!running&&!onBall&&!atReceiver){const gp=engine.groundAtScreen(point.x,point.y);drag.startRun=!!(gp&&runAt(gp));/* a touch begun on a run arrow is a through ball, never a shot */}e.preventDefault();
 if(running)$('gestureLabel').textContent='BALAYE SUR LE CÔTÉ : RÂTEAU · VERS L’AVANT : POUSSÉE';else if(!onBall)updateAim(point);else $('gestureLabel').textContent='TIRE EN ARRIÈRE · AJUSTE LA CIBLE · RELÂCHE';
}
canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',startGesture);
canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(drag&&drag.id===e.pointerId){if(drag.kind==='skill'){drag.current={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};drag.points.push({...drag.current});}else if(action?.ready)updateAim({x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY});e.preventDefault();}});
function cancelAim(){drag=null;if(engine){engine.aim.visible=false;if(engine.targetRing)engine.targetRing.visible=false;}}
canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancelAim);canvas.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',cancelAim);
canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
 if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;if(action?.ready)updateAim({x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY});const fwd=drag.start.y-e.clientY,side=Math.abs(e.clientX-drag.start.x);if(drag.kind==='skill'&&action?.phase==='dribble'&&fwd>45&&side<fwd*.8){const h=action.hero,end=action.dribbleEnd||h,dx=end.x-h.x,dz=end.z-h.z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz);cancelAim();beginPush(l>.2?{x:h.x+dx/l*7,z:h.z+dz/l*7}:{x:h.x,z:h.z-7});return;}
 const skill=lateralSkill([...drag.points,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY}],Date.now()-drag.began);if(skill&&rakeAllowed({x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY})&&(action?.ready||action?.phase==='dribble')){cancelAim();beginRake(skill.side);return;}if(!action?.ready){cancelAim();return;}updateAim({x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY});
 lastRaw=drag.raw||null;lastThrough=!!drag.through;const wasShot=drag.points.some(p=>p.y-drag.start.y>=18),bicycleKick=!!drag.bicycle&&fwd>45,tapPoint={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY},pull=Math.hypot(e.clientX-drag.start.x,e.clientY-drag.start.y),target=drag.target,spin=drag.spin||0,kind=drag.kind,selected=mode,power=drag.power,backPull=e.clientY-drag.start.y;cancelAim();
 if(action.phase==='aerial'&&bicycleKick){beginBicycle();return;}
 if(action.phase==='aerial'&&(selected==='dribble'||kind==='ball'&&pull<10&&!wasShot)){const touch=selected==='dribble'&&target;action.aerial=null;controlAgain(touch?'Contrôle orienté.':'Contrôle réussi · à toi de jouer.');if(touch)beginDribble(target,1.9);return;}
 if(kind==='ball'&&wasShot&&backPull<-25&&!action.setPiece&&action.phase==='ready'){beginFeint(target);return;}
 if(action.setPiece==='penalty'&&!(selected==='normal'&&kind==='ball'&&backPull>=18||selected==='normal'&&wasShot)){$('feedback').textContent='Penalty : glisse vers toi pour frapper.';return;}
 if(kind==='ball'&&pull<10&&!wasShot){const point=engine.groundAtScreen(tapPoint.x,tapPoint.y);if(point){beginDribble(point);return;}}
 if(!target||kind==='ball'&&backPull<18){$('feedback').textContent='Touche pour avancer. Pour tirer, glisse vers toi depuis une zone libre du terrain.';return;}
 if(selected==='dribble')beginDribble(target);else{action.throughRun=lastThrough?{mate:action.receiver,point:{...target}}:null;{const mate=action.receiver,others=(action.teammates||[]).filter(t=>t!==mate&&t.tacticalId%11>=8);const r=others.sort((p,q)=>p.z-q.z)[0];action.depthRun=selected!=='normal'&&r?{mate:r,point:{x:clamp(r.x*.5,-8,8),z:Math.max(-16,r.z-7)}}:null;}/* Lot 2 · a ball into space is played 2.5 m past the spot so it still has pace when the runner arrives */const h0=action.hero,ddx=target.x-h0.x,ddz=target.z-h0.z,dl=Math.hypot(ddx,ddz)||1,aimAt=lastThrough&&selected==='low'?{x:target.x+ddx/dl*2.5,z:target.z+ddz/dl*2.5}:target;windUp(action.hero,aimAt,selected,'hero',spin,selected==='normal'?power:null);}
});
let lastRaw=null,lastThrough=false;
const wrapAngle=a=>Math.atan2(Math.sin(a),Math.cos(a));
// Weak foot (8): the player is right-footed; a strike that turns clearly to his left uses the left foot.
function weakFootFor(p,target){if(!p||!target)return false;const ta=Math.atan2(-(target.x-p.x),-(target.z-p.z));return wrapAngle(ta-(p.angle||0))>.5;}
function weakFootSkill(){return clamp(career.weakFoot??.45,.3,.95);}
// Shield (7): back into the nearest defender, keep the ball on the far side and wait for support.
function beginShield(){if(setPieceLocked())return;const a=action;if(!a?.ready)return;cancelAim();a.started=true;a.ready=false;a.phase='shield';a.shieldTime=0;a.pressIndex=nearestDefender(a.hero);drainEnergy(.4);$('feedback').textContent='Tu protèges le ballon… un partenaire vient en soutien.';}
// Feint (5): pull back as for a shot and come back to the start before releasing.
function beginFeint(target){if(setPieceLocked())return;const a=action;a.started=true;a.feints=(a.feints||0)+1;const bite=[.85,.5,.22][Math.min(2,a.feints-1)],h=a.hero;let d=null,best=4.6;for(const q of a.defenders){const dist=Math.hypot(q.x-h.x,q.z-h.z);if(dist<best&&!q.challenge){best=dist;d=q;}}
 const fooled=d&&Math.random()<bite;if(fooled){beginChallenge(d,{x:h.x+(target?.x-h.x||0)*.08,y:R,z:h.z-.6},'block');d.challengeCooldown=0;}if(target&&Math.random()<bite*.7&&Math.hypot(h.x,h.z+20)<22)a.keeper.x=clamp(a.keeper.x+Math.sign(target.x-a.keeper.x||1)*.55,-3.2,3.2);
 a.ready=false;a.phase='feint';a.feintTime=0;h.kickTimer=.05;drainEnergy(.3);$('feedback').textContent=!d?'Feinte… personne n’a mordu.':fooled?'Feinte ! Le défenseur se jette, à toi d’en profiter.':a.feints>2?'Il ne mord plus à tes feintes.':'Feinte… le défenseur reste debout.';}
// Push (6): a long touch into space; the ball is loose and the player sprints after it.
function beginPush(point){if(setPieceLocked())return;const a=action,h=a.hero,dx=point.x-h.x,dz=point.z-h.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz),k=Math.min(1,7.5/len),to={x:h.x+dx*k,z:h.z+dz*k};a.started=true;a.ready=false;a.ball=launch({x:a.ball.x,z:a.ball.z},to,'low');a.kicker=h;a.shotBy='push';makeLoose('Poussée de balle ! Fonce dessus avant le défenseur.',h);drainEnergy(.8);match.dribbles++;}
function setShotCurve(value){shotCurve=value;for(const [id,n] of [['curveLeft',-.35],['curveNone',0],['curveRight',.35]])$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(Math.abs(value-n)<.001));const sl=$('curveSlider');if(sl)sl.value=String(Math.round(value/.4*100));const cv=$('curveValue');if(cv)cv.textContent=value===0?'Sans effet':(value<0?'↶ ':'')+Math.round(Math.abs(value)/.4*100)+' %'+(value>0?' ↷':'');if(drag)updateAim(drag.current);}
$('curveSlider')?.addEventListener('input',e=>setShotCurve(Number(e.target.value)/100*.4));
$('curveLeft').onclick=()=>setShotCurve(-.35);$('curveNone').onclick=()=>setShotCurve(0);$('curveRight').onclick=()=>setShotCurve(.35);
// Wide view and off-screen partners: look around without leaving the close camera for good.
let wideView=false;const mateArrows=new Map();
let look=null;
$('wideView').addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{look={x:e.clientX,moved:false};$('wideView').setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);e.preventDefault();});
$('wideView').addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!look)return;const dx=e.clientX-look.x;if(Math.abs(dx)>12)look.moved=true;if(look.moved)engine?.setLookYaw?.(clamp(-dx*.008,-1.1,1.1));});
$('wideView').addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{if(look&&!look.moved){wideView=!wideView;$('wideView').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(wideView));engine?.setWideView?.(wideView);}engine?.setLookYaw?.(0);look=null;});
$('wideView').addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{engine?.setLookYaw?.(0);look=null;});
function passToTeammate(t){const a=action;if(!a||!a.ready||a.ended||guideOpen||a.setPiece==='penalty'||!a.teammates.includes(t)||t.tacticalId%11===0)return;cancelAim();const d=Math.hypot(t.x-a.hero.x,t.z-a.hero.z);a.receiver=t;windUp(a.hero,{x:t.x,z:t.z},d>24?'lob':'low','hero');}
function updateOffscreen(){const box=$('offscreen');if(!box?.appendChild||typeof document.createElement!=='function'||!engine?.actorNDC)return;const a=action,seen=new Set();
 if(a&&a.phase!=='interval'&&!a.ended&&!match?.training){const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(),W=r.width,H=r.height;
  // Only real passing options: never the own goalkeeper, at most four, the closest first, within 28 m.
  const options=a.teammates.filter(t=>t.tacticalId%11!==0&&Math.hypot(t.x-a.hero.x,t.z-a.hero.z)<45).sort((p,q)=>Math.hypot(p.x-a.hero.x,p.z-a.hero.z)-Math.hypot(q.x-a.hero.x,q.z-a.hero.z));let shown=0;
  for(const t of options){const v=engine.actorNDC(t);if(!v.behind&&Math.abs(v.x)<.96&&Math.abs(v.y)<.96)continue;if(++shown>4)break;let x=v.behind?-v.x:v.x,y=v.behind?-1.2:v.y;const k=Math.max(Math.abs(x),Math.abs(y),1);x/=k;y/=k;
   const px=clamp((x+1)/2*W,28,W-28),py=clamp((1-y)/2*H,165,H-150);let el=mateArrows.get(t);
   if(!el){el=document.createElement('button');el.className='offscreen-mate';el.setAttribute('aria-label','Passer au numéro '+(t.number||''));el.innerHTML='<b>'+(t.number||'')+'</b><i>➜</i>';el.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();passToTeammate(t);});box.appendChild(el);mateArrows.set(t,el);}
   el.hidden=false;el.style.transform=`translate(${px-22}px,${py-22}px)`;el.lastChild.style.transform=`rotate(${Math.atan2(py-H/2,px-W/2)}rad)`;seen.add(t);}}
 for(const [t,el] of mateArrows){if(!a||!a.teammates.includes(t)){el.remove();mateArrows.delete(t);}else if(!seen.has(t))el.hidden=true;}}
$('soundToggle').onclick=()=>setSound(career.sound===false);$('soundHome').addEventListener('change',()=>setSound($('soundHome').checked));
$('crowdToggle').addEventListener('change',()=>{if(engine)engine.setCrowdVisible($('crowdToggle').checked);});
function showGuide(open){guideOpen=open;cancelAim();$('gestureGuide').hidden=!open;$('gestureHelp').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));}
// V19 · Secondary tools live in the ⋯ menu so the top of the pitch stays clear.
function showMenu(open){$('toolMenu').hidden=!open;$('menuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open));}
$('menuToggle').onclick=()=>showMenu($('toolMenu').hidden);$('toolMenu').addEventListener('click',()=>showMenu(false));
$('gestureHelp').onclick=()=>showGuide(!guideOpen);$('guideClose').onclick=()=>showGuide(false);
$('fullscreen').onclick=async()=>{try{if(document.fullscreenElement)await document.exitFullscreen();else if($('match').requestFullscreen)await $('match').requestFullscreen();}catch(e){$('feedback').textContent='Le terrain occupe déjà tout l’espace disponible dans ce navigateur.';}};
function nearestDefender(player){return action.defenders.reduce((best,d,i,ds)=>Math.hypot(d.x-player.x,d.z-player.z)<Math.hypot(ds[best].x-player.x,ds[best].z-player.z)?i:best,0);}
function windUp(player,target,kickMode,by,spin=0,power=null){const weakFoot=by==='hero'&&player===action.hero&&weakFootFor(player,target);learn(kickMode==='low'?'pass':kickMode==='lob'?'lob':Math.abs(spin)>.2?'spin':'shot');action.started=true;action.pressIndex=assignMarkers(action.defenders,player,[action.hero,...action.teammates]);action.ready=false;action.flying=false;action.phase='windup';action.windup={player,target:{...target},mode:kickMode,by,spin,power,elapsed:0,weak:weakFoot};action.passRecipient=kickMode==='normal'?null:by==='return'?action.hero:[action.hero,...action.teammates].filter(t=>t!==player).find(t=>Math.hypot(t.x-target.x,t.z-target.z)<1.5)||null;player.kickTimer=0;player.angle=Math.atan2(-(target.x-player.x),-(target.z-player.z));player.speed=0;action.shotBy=by;$('combination').hidden=true;$('feedback').textContent=by==='return'?'Ton coéquipier prépare la remise…':by==='teammate'?'Ton coéquipier arme sa frappe…':'Tu armes ton geste…';}
function releaseBall(){drainEnergy(.7);const w=action.windup,p=w.player;action.runCounted=false;if(w.by==='hero'&&w.mode==='normal'&&!match.training)match.shots=(match.shots||0)+1;action.ball=launch({x:p.x-Math.sin(p.angle)*.5,z:p.z-Math.cos(p.angle)*.5},w.target,w.mode,w.spin*attributes(developmentXP()).curve,w.power,attributes(developmentXP()).force*(.9+.1*match.energy/100));const weak=!!w.weak;if(w.mode!=='normal'){const pressed=action.defenders.some(d=>Math.hypot(d.x-p.x,d.z-p.z)<1.2);if(pressed){const b=action.ball,e=(Math.random()-.5)*.12,c=Math.cos(e),sn=Math.sin(e),vx=b.vx;b.vx=vx*c-b.vz*sn;b.vz=vx*sn+b.vz*c;}}if(!match.training&&action.teammates?.length&&(w.mode==='low'||w.mode==='lob')&&(w.by==='hero'||w.by==='teamPass')&&!action.setPiece){action.callPause=true;action.callWait=0;$('feedback').textContent='Passe partie : touche où tu veux courir, ou ton joueur pour rester.';}aerialStrike(action);releaseSetPiece(action);if(w.by==='return'&&action.combination){const ch=chemistryWith(p),b=action.ball,e=(Math.random()-.5)*.1*(1-ch),c=Math.cos(e),sn=Math.sin(e),vx=b.vx;b.vx=vx*c-b.vz*sn;b.vz=vx*sn+b.vz*c;}
 if(action.throughRun&&w.by==='hero'){const tr=action.throughRun,b=action.ball,sp=5.1*PACE,run=Math.hypot(tr.mate.x-tr.point.x,tr.mate.z-tr.point.z)/sp+sp/(2*10*PACE),dist=Math.hypot(b.x-tr.point.x,b.z-tr.point.z),v=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vz),want=clamp(dist/Math.max(run,.4)+1.2,6,v);b.vx*=want/v;b.vz*=want/v;}
 if(weak){const wf=weakFootSkill(),b=action.ball,err=(Math.random()-.5)*2*(1-wf)*.13,c=Math.cos(err),sn=Math.sin(err),vx=b.vx,f=.86+.14*wf;b.vx=(vx*c-b.vz*sn)*f;b.vz=(vx*sn+b.vz*c)*f;career.weakFoot=Math.min(.95,weakFootSkill()+(match.training?.012:.003));save();}action.offside=w.mode!=='normal'&&!action.setPiece&&['hero','teamPass','return'].includes(w.by)?offsideAtPass(p):null;action.kicker=p;action.assistEligible=w.by==='hero'&&w.mode!=='normal';action.loose=false;action.contactIgnore=null;action.flightTarget={...w.target};action.flightMode=w.mode;action.flying=true;action.phase='flight';action.flightTime=0;action.shotBy=w.by;action.followTarget={x:p.x-Math.sin(p.angle)*1,z:p.z-Math.cos(p.angle)*1};action.keeper.shotTime=0;$('feedback').textContent=w.by==='return'?'Une-deux : la remise revient vers toi…':w.by==='teammate'?'Regarde la frappe de ton coéquipier !':w.mode==='lob'?'Le ballon prend de la hauteur…':w.mode==='normal'?'Frappe partie !':'Passe partie : touche l’espace libre pour faire ton appel.';}
// Volleys and headers leave from the ball's height; they are harder to place than a strike on the ground.
// V19 · Bicycle kick: back to goal, the player throws himself up and strikes over his head. Spectacular, imprecise.
function beginBicycle(){const a=action,ae=a.aerial,h=a.hero;if(a.phase!=='aerial'||!ae||ae.ground)return false;const gx=-ae.x,gz=-20-ae.z,gl=Math.hypot(gx,gz)||1;h.x=ae.x+gx/gl*.3;h.z=ae.z+gz/gl*.3;ae.bicycle=true;ae.header=false;ae.jump=clamp(ae.y-1.5,0,.4);h.heading=false;h.bicycle=true;h.y=ae.jump;
 windUp(h,{x:(Math.random()-.5)*4.6,z:-21},'normal','hero',0,.86);h.angle+=Math.PI;drainEnergy(1.2);$('feedback').textContent='Retournée !';return true;}
function aerialStrike(a){const ae=a.aerial;if(!ae)return;a.aerial=null;const b=a.ball;b.x=ae.x;b.y=ae.y;b.z=ae.z;const err=(Math.random()-.5)*(ae.ground?.06:ae.header?.2:.14),c=Math.cos(err),s=Math.sin(err),vx=b.vx;b.vx=vx*c-b.vz*s;b.vz=vx*s+b.vz*c;
 if(ae.bicycle){const e2=(Math.random()-.5)*.3,c2=Math.cos(e2),s2=Math.sin(e2),vx2=b.vx;b.vx=(vx2*c2-b.vz*s2)*.85;b.vz=(vx2*s2+b.vz*c2)*.85;b.vy=.6+Math.random()*1.4;b.spin=0;a.hero.grounded=a.sim+.9;}
 else if(ae.ground){}else if(ae.header){b.vx*=.6;b.vz*=.6;b.vy=-1.2+b.vy*.25;b.spin=0;}else{b.vy=Math.min(b.vy,2.2)*.75-.4;}learn('shot');}
// Set pieces: the wall and the waiting players move only once the ball is struck; the keeper guesses a penalty.
// Free kicks: a lifted, dipping strike so the ball can clear a jumping wall and come down under the bar.
let FK_LIFT=5.4;
function releaseSetPiece(a){if(a.released)return;a.released=true;if(a.setPiece==='freekick'&&a.windup?.mode==='normal'&&a.windup.by==='hero'){const b=a.ball,f=.84;b.vx*=f;b.vz*=f;b.vy=Math.max(b.vy,0)*.55+FK_LIFT;b.dip=true;}for(const d of a.defenders){d.hold=false;if(d.wall){d.wallUntil=a.sim+.75;d.jumpAt=a.sim+.06;}}for(const t of a.teammates)t.hold=false;
 if(a.setPiece==='penalty'&&a.windup?.by==='hero'){const k=a.keeper,g=Math.random();k.noReact=true;if(g<.78){k.state='dive';k.elapsed=0;k.dir=g<.39?-1:1;k.diveStartX=k.x;k.jump=.55;}$('feedback').textContent='Frappe partie… le gardien a choisi !';}a.setPiece=null;}
function beginRake(side){if(setPieceLocked())return;sideLearn(side);drainEnergy(1);learn('rake');const h=action.hero;action.started=true;action.ready=false;action.phase='skill';action.dribbles++;action.pressIndex=assignMarkers(action.defenders,h,action.teammates);action.skill={side,time:0,duration:.62,start:{x:h.x,z:h.z},ballStart:{x:action.ball.x,z:action.ball.z},end:{x:clamp(h.x+side*1.7*PACE,-PITCH.W+1,PITCH.W-1),z:clamp(h.z-.55,-19.3,PITCH.far-1)}};h.skillTimer=0;h.skillSide=side;h.angle=0;$('feedback').textContent='Râteau '+(side<0?'à gauche':'à droite')+' : protège le ballon !';$('gestureLabel').textContent='RÂTEAU';}
function sideLearn(dx){if(Math.abs(dx)<.6||match.training)return;match.sideLeft=(match.sideLeft||0)+(dx<0);match.sideRight=(match.sideRight||0)+(dx>0);const n=match.sideLeft+match.sideRight;match.sideBias=(match.sideRight-match.sideLeft)/(n+4);}
function beginDribble(target,maxLen=null){if(setPieceLocked())return;{const h=action.hero,dx=target.x-h.x,dz=target.z-h.z,ang=Math.atan2(dx,dz);sideLearn(dx);if(action.lastDribbleAngle!=null&&Math.abs(wrapAngle(ang-action.lastDribbleAngle))>1.05){const d=action.defenders[action.pressIndex];if(d&&Math.hypot(d.x-h.x,d.z-h.z)<3&&Math.random()<.55){d.stun=.35;$('feedback').textContent='Changement de direction : le défenseur est surpris.';}}action.lastDribbleAngle=ang;}learn('dribble');action.started=true;action.ready=false;action.phase='dribble';action.dribbleEnd=dribbleTarget(action.hero,target);{const h=action.hero,close=action.defenders.some(d=>Math.hypot(d.x-h.x,d.z-h.z)<2.4),e=action.dribbleEnd,len=Math.hypot(e.x-h.x,e.z-h.z);const cap=Math.min(maxLen??9,close?2.1:9);if(len>cap){e.x=h.x+(e.x-h.x)/len*cap;e.z=h.z+(e.z-h.z)/len*cap;}}action.dribbleElapsed=0;action.dribbles++;action.pressIndex=assignMarkers(action.defenders,action.hero,action.teammates);$('feedback').textContent='Tu conduis le ballon. Attention au défenseur !';}
function attachBall(player,moving=false){const step=moving?.58+Math.sin(action.sim*17)*.16:.58;action.ball={x:player.x-Math.sin(player.angle)*step,y:R+(moving?Math.abs(Math.sin(action.sim*17))*.055:0),z:player.z-Math.cos(player.angle)*step,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,bounces:0,age:0};}
function handOffIncoming(){const a=action;a.incomingPass=true;a.phase='flight';a.flying=true;a.loose=false;a.flightTime=0;a.contactIgnore=null;a.keeper.shotTime=0;}
function clearCross(d){const a=action,b=a.ball;d.kickTimer=.1;Object.assign(b,{vx:(b.x-d.x)*2.5+(Math.random()-.5)*3,vy:3.2,vz:6.5,spin:0});a.kicker=d;a.runTarget=null;makeLoose('Le défenseur dégage le centre de la tête !',d);}
function controlAgain(message){if(action&&!match.training&&['incoming','flight'].includes(action.phase)&&!action.loose){const h=action.hero,near=action.defenders.some(d=>Math.hypot(d.x-h.x,d.z-h.z)<1.4);if(near&&Math.random()<(match.scenario==='pluie'?.2:.13)){const ang=h.angle||0;action.ball={x:h.x,y:R,z:h.z,vx:-Math.sin(ang)*4,vy:0,vz:-Math.cos(ang)*4,age:0,bounces:0};action.kicker=h;makeLoose('Contrôle trop long sous la pression !',h);return;}}if(['dribble','skill'].includes(action.phase))match.dribbles++;action.runTarget=null;action.loose=false;action.contactIgnore=null;action.possessionTime=0;action.hero.heading=false;action.hero.bicycle=false;action.hero.grounded=0;action.pressureWarned=false;action.pressIndex=nearestDefender(action.hero);action.phase='ready';action.ready=true;action.flying=false;action.hero.speed=0;action.hero.kickTimer=-1;action.hero.skillTimer=-1;attachBall(action.hero);action.keeper.shotTime=0;setMode('normal');updateDistance();$('feedback').textContent=message;}
function chemistryWith(t){return Math.min(1,((career.chem||{})[t?.number]||0)/40);}
function receivePass(){if(isOffsidePlayer(action.receiver)){callOffside(action.receiver);return;}action.throughRun=null;if(action.shotBy==='hero'&&!action.loose&&action.receiver?.number&&!match.training){career.chem=career.chem||{};career.chem[action.receiver.number]=Math.min(60,(career.chem[action.receiver.number]||0)+1);}const lowReception=action.ball.y<.75;if(action.shotBy==='hero'&&!action.loose)match.passes++;action.assistFromHero=action.shotBy==='hero';action.loose=false;action.possessionTime=0;action.pressureWarned=false;action.pressIndex=assignMarkers(action.defenders,action.receiver,[action.hero,...action.teammates]);action.flying=false;action.ready=false;action.phase='choice';action.decisionTime=0;action.receiver.speed=0;action.receiver.angle=0;attachBall(action.receiver);$('combination').hidden=false;$('prompt').textContent='Fais ton appel.';$('feedback').textContent='Touche l’espace libre pour courir et recevoir la remise, ou touche le but pour le laisser tirer.';const immediate=teamDecision(action.receiver,[action.hero,...action.teammates],action.defenders,action.keeper);if(!match.training&&immediate.kind==='shot'&&Math.hypot(action.receiver.x,action.receiver.z+20)<10&&lowReception){action.runTarget=null;playTeamDecision(immediate,lowReception);return;}if(action.runTarget){action.phase='offball';action.runTime=0;$('combination').hidden=true;}else if(!match.training){const decision=teamDecision(action.receiver,[action.hero,...action.teammates],action.defenders,action.keeper);if(decision.kind==='shot'){$('feedback').textContent='Ton partenaire contrôle avant de frapper.';$('combination').hidden=true;}else{$('combination').hidden=true;$('feedback').textContent='Ton partenaire cherche une solution. Touche l’espace libre pour faire ton appel.';}}}
function playTeamDecision(decision,oneTouch=false){const a=action,carrier=a.receiver;if(!carrier)return;
 if(decision.kind==='shot'){if(a.assistFromHero)match.chances++;a.goalAssist=a.assistFromHero;windUp(carrier,decision.target,'normal','teammate',0,clamp((decision.power??.65)+(Math.random()-.5)*.06,.58,.78));if(oneTouch){a.windup.elapsed=.23;carrier.kickTimer=.23;}$('feedback').textContent=oneTouch?'Reprise en une touche !':'Ton partenaire frappe.';}
 else if(decision.kind==='pass'||decision.kind==='cross'){a.goalAssist=false;const to=decision.receiver;windUp(carrier,decision.target,decision.kind==='cross'||decision.lob?'lob':'low',to===a.hero?'return':'teamPass');a.passRecipient=to;a.lastTeamPasser=carrier;to.lastPasser=carrier;if(to===a.hero){a.returnSpot={...decision.target};a.combination=true;}$('feedback').textContent='Ton partenaire fait circuler vers un joueur disponible.';}
 else{a.phase='teammateRun';a.teamTarget=decision.target;a.carryTime=0;}}
function chooseCombination(returnPass){if(action?.phase!=='choice')return;$('combination').hidden=true;action.combination=returnPass;if(returnPass){const h=action.hero;action.returnSpot={x:clamp(h.x+2,-PITCH.W+1,PITCH.W-1),z:clamp(h.z-2.2,-17,PITCH.far-3)};windUp(action.receiver,action.returnSpot,'low','return');}else playTeamDecision(teamDecision(action.receiver,[action.hero,...action.teammates],action.defenders,action.keeper));}
$('oneTwo').onclick=()=>chooseCombination(true);$('teammateShot').onclick=()=>chooseCombination(false);
function resolve(success,message,scorer=action?.shotBy,kind=success?'goal':'miss'){
 if(!action||action.ended)return;cancelAim();const a=action;a.ended=true;a.ready=false;a.flying=false;a.phase='ended';a.after=0;a.outcomeKind=kind;
 if(kind==='goal'){containGoalBall(a.ball);a.ballControl='net';}
 else if(kind==='save'){a.ballControl='held';a.keeper.holding=true;Object.assign(a.ball,keeperGrip(a.keeper),{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0});}
 else if(kind==='tackle'||kind==='offside'){a.ballControl='dead';Object.assign(a.ball,{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,y:R});}
 else a.ballControl='loose';
 if(kind==='tackle')match.losses++;if(success){match.success++;match.us++;if(!match.training)match.events.push({minute:Math.floor(match.minute),side:'us',applied:true,scorer:scorer==='teammate'?(a.receiver?.name||'Un partenaire'):career.name});if(scorer==='teammate'){if(a.goalAssist!==false)match.assists++;}else if(scorer==='hero'||scorer==='return')match.goals++;a.hero.celebrate=true;for(const t of a.teammates||[a.receiver])if(t)t.celebrate=true;if(!match.training)startCelebration(a,scorer);say(scorer==='teammate'?(a.goalAssist!==false?'assist':'goal_us'):'hero_goal');}
 else if(kind==='save')say('save');else if(kind==='tackle')say('tackle');else if(kind==='miss'&&a.shotBy==='hero')say('miss');
 $('combination').hidden=true;$('feedback').textContent=message;updateScore();$('continue').textContent='Reprendre le match →';$('continue').hidden=true;
}
// 29 · V19 celebration cinematic, 5–10 s: the scorer runs to the fans behind the goal and performs one of 15 routines
// drawn at random. After a figure the team comes afterwards; otherwise they join him straight away. Still behind
// after the goal: no time to celebrate, he fetches the ball from the net.
const POSE_OF={glissade:'slide'};
// The curve control is useless once the action is over: a countdown shows how long the replay stays available.
function updateReplayTimer(){const a=action,on=!!(a?.ended&&!match?.training&&!replay&&a.tape?.length>=TAPE_HZ);$('match').classList.toggle('action-over',on);if($('replayTimer').hidden===on)$('replayTimer').hidden=!on;if(!on)return;const total=continueDelay(a),rem=Math.max(0,total-a.after),txt=Math.ceil(rem)+' s';if($('replayTimeText').textContent!==txt)$('replayTimeText').textContent=txt;$('replayTimeBar').style.width=(rem/total*100).toFixed(1)+'%';}
function continueDelay(a){return a.celebration?.duration??(a.outcomeKind==='goal'?3.8:2.6);}
function startCelebration(a,scorer){const by=scorer==='teammate'&&a.receiver?a.receiver:a.hero,behind=match.us<match.them;
 const c=behind?{id:'ball',label:'VITE, LE BALLON !',kind:'ball',duration:3.2}:{...pickCelebration(Math.random),duration:5+Math.random()*5};
 c.scorer=by;c.time=0;c.phase='run';c.target=behind?{x:0,z:-19.3}:{x:clamp(by.x*.5,-9,9)+(Math.sign(by.x)||1)*4,z:-18.4};a.celebration=c;a.cinematic=!behind;
 for(const t of [a.hero,...a.teammates])t.celebrate=behind?false:t===by?false:true;announce('BUT !',match.us+' — '+match.them,2.2);}
function celebrate(a,p,dt){const c=a.celebration;if(!c){p.speed=0;return;}
 if(p===c.scorer){c.time+=dt;
  if(c.kind==='ball'){moveToward(p,c.target,5.2,dt);return;}
  if(c.phase==='run'){const d=Math.hypot(p.x-c.target.x,p.z-c.target.z);if(c.id==='glissade'&&d<3.2){p.celebrate='slide';moveToward(p,c.target,Math.max(.8,d*1.6),dt);}else moveToward(p,c.target,5,dt);
   if(d<.45||c.time>2.6){c.phase='perform';c.performAt=c.time;p.angle=0;p.speed=0;p.celebrate=POSE_OF[c.id]||c.id;}return;}
  const t=c.time-c.performAt;p.celebrateTime=t;
  if(c.phase==='perform'){
   if(c.id==='salto')p.y=t<1.1?1.1*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/1.1):0;else if(c.id==='vrille')p.y=t<.9?.65*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/.9):0;else if(c.id==='siu')p.y=t<.62?.6*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/.62):0;else if(c.id==='griddy'||c.id==='pouce')p.y=Math.abs(Math.sin(t*(c.id==='griddy'?8:5)))*.05;
   p.speed=0;
   if(t>(c.kind==='figure'?2.2:3.2)){c.phase='group';p.y=0;}return;}
  p.speed=0;p.y=0;p.celebrate=true;return;}
 // Team-mates: arms up where they are, then they run to the scorer and stop just short of him.
 if(c.kind==='ball'||c.kind==='figure'&&c.phase!=='group'){p.speed=0;return;}
 const s=c.scorer,d=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.z-s.z);if(d>1.3&&d<40)moveToward(p,{x:s.x+(p.x-s.x)/d*1.1,z:s.z+(p.z-s.z)/d*1.1},5,dt);else p.speed=0;}
// 30 · Optional replay: the action is recorded (decision pauses excluded) and can be watched again,
// at normal speed and from behind the goal. Nothing in the match moves while it plays.
const TAPE_HZ=30,TAPE_MAX=270;let replay=null;
function snapActor(p){return p&&{x:p.x,z:p.z,y:p.y||0,angle:p.angle,speed:p.speed,kickTimer:p.kickTimer,skillTimer:p.skillTimer,skillSide:p.skillSide,challenge:p.challenge?{time:p.challenge.time,kind:p.challenge.kind}:null,celebrate:p.celebrate,state:p.state,tilt:p.tilt,holding:p.holding};}
function recordFrame(a,dt){if(!a||a.phase==='interval'||a.callPause||a.ready&&!a.ended||a.ended&&a.after>1.8)return;a.tapeClock=(a.tapeClock||0)+dt;if(a.tapeClock<1/TAPE_HZ)return;a.tapeClock=0;const tape=a.tape||(a.tape=[]);tape.push({ball:{...a.ball},hero:snapActor(a.hero),teammates:(a.teammates||[]).map(snapActor),defenders:a.defenders.map(snapActor),keeper:snapActor(a.keeper),phase:a.phase,flying:a.flying,ballControl:a.ballControl,after:a.after,outcomeKind:a.outcomeKind,ended:a.ended});if(tape.length>TAPE_MAX)tape.shift();}
// V19 · Replay player: watch as often as you like, move through it and switch camera. Normal speed only.
const REPLAY_CAMS={goal:'rpCamGoal',side:'rpCamSide',hero:'rpCamHero',air:'rpCamAir'};let replayCam='goal';
function startReplay(){const a=action;if(!a?.ended||replay||!(a.tape?.length>=TAPE_HZ))return;replay={frames:a.tape.slice(),t:0,playing:true};engine.replayPos=null;for(const id of ['replayBtn','continue','offscreen','replayTimer'])$(id).hidden=true;$('replayBadge').hidden=false;$('replayPanel').hidden=false;$('match').classList.toggle('replaying',true);showReplayCam();}
const replayLength=()=>(replay.frames.length-1)/TAPE_HZ;
function seekReplay(t,play){if(!replay)return;replay.t=clamp(t,0,replayLength());if(play!=null)replay.playing=play;}
function showReplayCam(){for(const [cam,id] of Object.entries(REPLAY_CAMS))$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(cam===replayCam));}
$('rpPlay').onclick=()=>{if(!replay)return;if(!replay.playing&&replay.t>=replayLength()-.01)replay.t=0;replay.playing=!replay.playing;};$('rpRestart').onclick=()=>seekReplay(0,true);$('rpBack').onclick=()=>seekReplay((replay?.t||0)-2);$('rpFwd').onclick=()=>seekReplay((replay?.t||0)+2);
$('replayScrub').addEventListener('input',e=>{if(replay)seekReplay(Number(e.target.value)/1000*replayLength(),false);});
for(const [cam,id] of Object.entries(REPLAY_CAMS))$(id).onclick=()=>{replayCam=cam;engine.replayPos=null;showReplayCam();};
function playReplay(dt){const max=replayLength();if(replay.playing){replay.t+=dt;if(replay.t>=max){replay.t=max;replay.playing=false;}}const f=replay.frames[Math.min(replay.frames.length-1,Math.floor(replay.t*TAPE_HZ))];engine.replayView?.(f,dt,replayCam);engine.render(f,dt,time);
 $('replayScrub').value=String(Math.round(replay.t/Math.max(max,.001)*1000));const label=replay.playing?'⏸':'▶';if($('rpPlay').textContent!==label)$('rpPlay').textContent=label;}
function stopReplay(){if(!replay)return;replay=null;$('replayBadge').hidden=true;$('replayPanel').hidden=true;$('match').classList.toggle('replaying',false);$('offscreen').hidden=false;if(action?.ended){$('replayBtn').hidden=false;if(!match?.training)$('continue').hidden=false;action.after=Math.min(action.after,1.2);}}
$('replayBtn').onclick=startReplay;$('replaySkip').onclick=stopReplay;
// V19 · A slide either takes the ball (won or deflected), catches the player first (foul) or misses: the defender is then on the ground.
function slideContact(d){const a=action,c=d.challenge;if(c?.kind!=='slide'||a.ended||c.done)return false;
 // Lot 3 · From behind, the legs are taken before the ball: any contact with the player is a foul, and the slide stops at his body.
 {const h=a.hero,hv=Math.hypot(h.vx||0,h.vz||0),fx=hv>.3?(h.vx||0)/hv:-Math.sin(h.angle||0),fz=hv>.3?(h.vz||0)/hv:-Math.cos(h.angle||0),behind=c.dx*fx+c.dz*fz>.45;if(behind&&c.time>.04&&c.time<.6&&Math.hypot(d.x+c.dx*.45-h.x,d.z+c.dz*.45-h.z)<.95){c.done=true;commitFoul(d);return true;}}
 if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)){c.done=true;if(Math.random()<.6)winBall(d,'Tacle glissé propre : il t’a pris le ballon.');else{Object.assign(a.ball,{vx:c.dx*6+(Math.random()-.5)*4,vy:.4,vz:c.dz*6+(Math.random()-.5)*4});makeLoose('Tacle glissé ! Le ballon file, va le chercher.',d);}return true;}
 if(c.time>.06&&c.time<.5&&Math.hypot(d.x+c.dx*.5-a.hero.x,d.z+c.dz*.5-a.hero.z)<.62){c.done=true;commitFoul(d);return true;}return false;}
// Foul: free kick (taken where it happened) or penalty in the box; a card for the defender, a red one when he was the last man.
function commitFoul(d){const a=action,h=a.hero,inBox=Math.abs(h.x)<20.16&&h.z<-3.5&&h.z>-20,lastMan=!a.defenders.some(q=>q!==d&&q.z<h.z-.5&&Math.abs(q.x-h.x)<14),key=d.tacticalId??'d'+a.defenders.indexOf(d);
 match.cards=match.cards||{};const prev=match.cards[key]||0;let card=null;if(lastMan&&!inBox||Math.random()<.06)card='red';else if(Math.random()<(d.style==='engagé'?.5:.35))card=prev?'red':'yellow';if(card)match.cards[key]=card==='red'?2:prev+1;
 match.foulsWon=(match.foulsWon||0)+1;if(card==='yellow')match.yellows=(match.yellows||0)+1;if(card==='red')match.reds=(match.reds||0)+1;
 for(const q of a.defenders){q.challenge=null;q.shoulder=false;q.lean=0;}h.lean=0;
 // Lot 2 · The referee runs to the culprit and shows the card: a short close-up before the free kick.
 if(card){const side=d.x>0?-1:1,c={d,card,inBox,t:0,rx:d.x+side*7,rz:d.z+5,rangle:0,rspeed:0,raised:false,name:d.name||'Le défenseur'};a.cardShow=c;a.phase='card';a.ready=false;a.flying=false;a.loose=false;a.runTarget=null;Object.assign(a.ball,{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0});ambience.whistle?.(2);$('feedback').textContent='Coup de sifflet… l’arbitre arrive.';d.speed=0;h.speed=0;return;}
 finishFoul(d,card,inBox);}
function tickCard(a,dt){const c=a.cardShow,d=c.d;c.t+=dt;a.frozen=true;const spot={x:d.x+(c.rx<d.x?-1.3:1.3),z:d.z+.6},dx=spot.x-c.rx,dz=spot.z-c.rz,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
 if(l>.05&&c.t<1.6){const v=Math.min(l,5.2*dt);c.rx+=dx/l*v;c.rz+=dz/l*v;c.rspeed=5.2;c.rangle=Math.atan2(-dx,-dz);}else{c.rspeed=0;c.rangle=Math.atan2(-(d.x-c.rx),-(d.z-c.rz));}
 d.angle=Math.atan2(-(c.rx-d.x),-(c.rz-d.z));
 if(!c.raised&&c.t>1.9){c.raised=true;announce(c.card==='red'?'🟥 CARTON ROUGE':'🟨 CARTON JAUNE',c.name+(c.card==='red'?' est expulsé':' est averti'),1.8);say(c.card==='red'?'red':'foul',{team:'L’adversaire'});}
 if(c.t>3.6){a.cardShow=null;finishFoul(d,c.card,c.inBox,true);}}
function finishFoul(d,card,inBox,shown=false){const a=action,h=a.hero;
 if(card==='red'){a.defenders.splice(a.defenders.indexOf(d),1);const p=match.tactical?.players.find(p=>p.id===d.tacticalId);if(p)p.out=true;}
 a.ball={x:h.x,y:R,z:h.z,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0};a.loose=false;a.flying=false;a.released=false;a.runTarget=null;
 if(inBox)setupPenalty();else setupFreeKick(30,14);a.chanceKind=inBox?'penalty':'freekick';a.phase='ready';a.ready=true;a.started=true;h.speed=0;h.kickTimer=-1;a.pressIndex=nearestDefender(h);engine.setup(a);engine.resize();
 $('kind').textContent=inBox?'PENALTY':'COUP FRANC';if(!shown){announce('FAUTE',(d.name||'Le défenseur')+' te fauche · '+(inBox?'penalty !':'coup franc'),2.4);say('foul',{team:'L’adversaire'});}}
// V19 · Offside: judged when the pass is played. A team-mate (or the player himself on a return pass) in the opposing
// half, ahead of the ball and beyond the second-last defender (the keeper counts) is offside if he then plays the ball.
function offsideLineZ(a){const zs=[a.keeper,...a.defenders].filter(Boolean).map(p=>p.z).sort((p,q)=>p-q);return zs[1]??GOAL_Z_LINE;}
const GOAL_Z_LINE=-20,HALFWAY=(PITCH.far-20)/2;
function offsideAtPass(passer){const a=action;if(match.training)return null;const line=offsideLineZ(a),ballZ=a.ball.z,players=[a.hero,...a.teammates].filter(p=>p&&p!==passer&&p.z<HALFWAY&&p.z<ballZ-.3&&p.z<line-.25);return players.length?{players,line}:null;}
const isOffsidePlayer=p=>!!action.offside?.players.includes(p);
function callOffside(p){const a=action;a.flag=true;match.offsides=(match.offsides||0)+1;const d=[...a.defenders,a.keeper].filter(Boolean).reduce((b,q)=>Math.hypot(q.x-a.ball.x,q.z-a.ball.z)<Math.hypot(b.x-a.ball.x,b.z-a.ball.z)?q:b);a.newOwner=d.tacticalId!=null?d:a.keeper;a.escapeTarget={x:a.newOwner.x,z:a.newOwner.z+1.5};ambience.whistle(1);say('offside',{who:p===a.hero?career.name:p.name||'ton partenaire'});resolve(false,(p===a.hero?'Hors-jeu ! Tu étais parti trop tôt.':'Hors-jeu de '+(p.name||'ton partenaire')+' !')+' Le drapeau se lève : coup franc adverse.',a.shotBy,'offside');}
function deflectBall(defender){const c=defender.challenge,ball=action.ball,dot=ball.vx*c.dx+ball.vz*c.dz;ball.vx-=1.6*dot*c.dx;ball.vz-=1.6*dot*c.dz;ball.vy=Math.max(ball.vy,1.6);makeLoose('Contré ! Le ballon reste en jeu : cours vers lui.',defender);}
function makeLoose(message,ignore=null){action.offside=null;action.incomingPass=false;action.ball.spin=0;action.runTarget=null;action.combination=false;action.followTarget=null;action.loose=true;action.flying=true;action.phase='flight';action.ballControl='loose';action.outcomeKind='deflection';action.contactIgnore=ignore;action.ignoreUntil=action.sim+.22;action.ball.age=0;action.looseTime=0;$('feedback').textContent=message;}
function winBall(defender,message){defender.challenge=null;resolve(false,message,action.shotBy,'tackle');action.newOwner=defender;action.ballControl='defender';defender.kickTimer=.18;action.escapeTarget={x:clamp(defender.x+(defender.x<action.hero.x?-1:1),-PITCH.W+1,PITCH.W-1),z:defender.z+2};defender.angle=Math.atan2(-(action.escapeTarget.x-defender.x),-(action.escapeTarget.z-defender.z));attachBall(defender);}
function patrol(p,i,dt){const t=action.sim;moveToward(p,{x:p.home.x+Math.sin(t*.85+i)*.6,z:p.home.z+Math.sin(t*.65+i)*.4},.75,dt);}
function updateActors(a,dt){if(a.phase==='press')return;
 for(const p of [a.hero,...(a.teammates||[a.receiver]),...a.defenders]){if(p)p.previous={x:p.x,z:p.z};if(p&&p.kickTimer>=0)p.kickTimer+=dt;}
 const carrier=['choice','offball','teammateRun'].includes(a.phase)?a.receiver:a.phase==='windup'?a.windup.player:a.hero;
 if(!a.ended){a.defenceClock=(a.defenceClock||0)-dt;if(a.defenceClock<=0){a.pressIndex=refreshDefence(a.defenders,carrier,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.pressIndex);a.defenceClock=.18;}}
 const supporter=a.phase==='shield'?(a.teammates||[]).filter(t=>t.tacticalId%11!==0).reduce((b,t)=>!b||Math.hypot(t.x-a.hero.x,t.z-a.hero.z)<Math.hypot(b.x-a.hero.x,b.z-a.hero.z)?t:b,null):null;
 const looseChase=a.flying&&a.loose?[a.defenders,a.shotBy==='push'?[]:a.teammates].map(group=>group.reduce((best,p)=>!best||Math.hypot(p.x-a.ball.x,p.z-a.ball.z)<Math.hypot(best.x-a.ball.x,best.z-a.ball.z)?p:best,null)):[null,null];
 for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++){
  const d=a.defenders[i];d.y=0;if(!a.ended&&d.hold&&!a.released){d.speed=0;continue;}if(!a.ended&&d.wallUntil&&a.sim<d.wallUntil){const t=a.sim-d.jumpAt;d.y=t>0&&t<.5?.3*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/.5):0;d.speed=0;continue;}if(a.ended){if(d.challenge)updateChallenge(d,dt);if(d===a.newOwner&&a.after<1.1)moveToward(d,a.escapeTarget,2.7,dt);else d.speed=0;continue;}
  if(updateChallenge(d,dt))continue;const distance=Math.hypot(d.x-a.ball.x,d.z-a.ball.z);
  if(d===looseChase[0]&&a.ball.y<1.6&&distance>1){moveToward(d,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.18,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.18},4.3*match.profile.press,dt,10);continue;}
  if((a.phase==='dribble'||a.phase==='skill')&&(i===a.pressIndex||distance<match.profile.reach*.8)&&distance<match.profile.reach*(DEF_STYLE[d.style]?.reach||1)){if(beginChallenge(d,{x:a.ball.x+(a.hero.vx||0)*.3,z:a.ball.z+(a.hero.vz||0)*.3},'poke'))continue;/* Lot 2 · the stab goes where the ball will be, not where it was */}
  // V19 · Slide tackle from 1.4–3.4 m, aimed where the ball is going. Committed defenders try it far more often.
  if((a.phase==='dribble'||a.phase==='skill')&&!d.challenge&&!(d.challengeCooldown>0)&&distance>1.4&&distance<3.4){const hv=Math.hypot(a.hero.vx||0,a.hero.vz||0),fromBehind=hv>.5&&((a.hero.x-d.x)*(a.hero.vx||0)+(a.hero.z-d.z)*(a.hero.vz||0))/(hv*distance)>.5,rate=(d.style==='engagé'?.55:d.style==='anticipe'?.28:.12)*match.profile.press*(fromBehind?.25:1);if(Math.random()<dt*rate&&beginChallenge(d,{x:a.ball.x+(a.hero.vx||0)*.3,y:R,z:a.ball.z+(a.hero.vz||0)*.3},'slide'))continue;}
  // V19 · Shoulder duel: a defender who catches the carrier from behind or the side runs with him, shoulder to shoulder.
  d.shoulder=false;d.lean=0;if(a.phase==='dribble'&&!d.challenge){const h=a.hero,sx=d.x-h.x,sz=d.z-h.z,sd=Math.hypot(sx,sz)||1,gx=-h.x,gz=-20-h.z,gl=Math.hypot(gx,gz)||1;if(sd<1.25&&(sx*gx+sz*gz)/(sd*gl)<.35){d.shoulder=true;const side=Math.sign(sx*gz-sz*gx)||1;d.lean=-side*.22;moveToward(d,{x:h.x+sx/sd*.78+(h.vx||0)*.12,z:h.z+sz/sd*.78+(h.vz||0)*.12},4.9,dt,14);continue;}}
  if(a.flying&&!a.incomingPass&&distance<1.6&&a.ball.y<1.65){if(beginChallenge(d,a.ball,'block'))continue;}
  const intent=a.phase==='dribble'?a.dribbleEnd:a.phase==='skill'?a.skill.end:a.phase==='teammateRun'?a.teamTarget:carrier;
  if(d.stun>0){d.stun-=dt;d.speed=0;continue;}const st=DEF_STYLE[d.style]||DEF_STYLE.prudent,lead=intent&&intent!==carrier?{x:carrier.x+(intent.x-carrier.x)*st.lead,z:carrier.z+(intent.z-carrier.z)*st.lead}:intent;
  let target=defensiveTarget(d,carrier,a.ball,i===a.pressIndex,a.flying,lead||carrier);
  // Lot 4 · On a cross to the player his marker contests from the goal side, shoulder to shoulder, instead of running through him to the ball.
  if(a.phase==='incoming'&&a.incomingMode==='lob'&&i===a.pressIndex){const h=a.hero,sd=Math.sign(d.x-h.x)||1;target={x:h.x+sd*.75,z:h.z-.55};}if(i===a.pressIndex&&carrier===a.hero)target.x+=(match.sideBias||0)*.7;
  const beaten=carrier&&d.z>carrier.z+.5&&Math.hypot(d.x-carrier.x,d.z-carrier.z)<9?1.2:1;/* Lot 2 · a beaten defender sprints back */moveToward(d,target,(i===a.pressIndex?4.35:3.8)*st.speed*beaten*match.profile.press*(a.phase==='windup'?.55:1),dt,9);
 }
 for(const t of a.teammates||[]){if(a.ended){celebrate(a,t,dt);continue;}if(t===carrier)continue;if(t.hold&&!a.released){t.speed=0;continue;}
  if(a.throughRun?.mate===t&&(a.phase==='windup'||a.flying&&!a.loose)){const tr=a.throughRun;if(!tr.attack&&a.flying&&Math.hypot(t.x-tr.point.x,t.z-tr.point.z)<1.2)tr.attack=true;/* at the spot he attacks the ball */moveToward(t,tr.attack?{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.15,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.15}:tr.point,5.1,dt,10);continue;}
  if(a.depthRun?.mate===t&&(a.phase==='windup'||a.flying||a.phase==='choice')){moveToward(t,a.depthRun.point,4.8,dt,10);continue;}
  {const run=a.runs?.find(r=>r.mate===t);if(run&&!a.loose&&['dribble','skill','windup','flight'].includes(a.phase)&&t!==a.passRecipient){moveToward(t,run.point,4.7,dt,10);continue;}}
  if(a.phase==='shield'&&t===supporter){const dx=t.x-a.hero.x,dz=t.z-a.hero.z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;moveToward(t,{x:a.hero.x+dx/l*4.5,z:a.hero.z+dz/l*4.5},4.6,dt,10);continue;}if(t===looseChase[1]&&a.ball.y<1.8&&Math.hypot(t.x-a.ball.x,t.z-a.ball.z)<14){moveToward(t,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.18,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.18},4.5*fatigueFactor(100),dt,10);continue;}if(t===a.passRecipient&&(a.flying||a.phase==='windup')){const target=a.flying?a.flightTarget:a.windup.target;moveToward(t,target,3.6,dt,8);continue;}
  const nearest=a.defenders.reduce((best,d)=>!best||Math.hypot(d.x-t.x,d.z-t.z)<Math.hypot(best.x-t.x,best.z-t.z)?d:best,null);
  // Offer width and depth; move away from a close marker instead of wandering.
  const away=nearest&&Math.hypot(nearest.x-t.x,nearest.z-t.z)<2?Math.sign(t.x-nearest.x)||1:0;
  // Possession: partners come closer to offer short options. Counter-attack: they sprint forward.
  const tt=teamTactic(),target={x:clamp(t.home.x+away*1.7+(carrier.x-t.home.x)*(tt==='possession'?.22:.08),-PITCH.W+1.5,PITCH.W-1.5),z:clamp(t.home.z+(t.support?-.5:-1.4)+clamp((carrier.z-a.start.z)*.65,-8,8)-(tt==='contre'&&!t.support?1.5:0),-18,PITCH.far-2)};
  moveToward(t,target,2.7*(tt==='contre'?1.2:1),dt,8);
 }

 // No two character bodies may occupy the same space, including after a tackle.
 for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){
  for(const d of a.defenders){separateBodies(d,a.hero,.82,true);for(const t of a.teammates||[a.receiver])if(t)separateBodies(d,t,.82,true);}
  for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<a.defenders.length;j++)separateBodies(a.defenders[i],a.defenders[j],.82);
 }

 if(a.ended&&a.celebration)celebrate(a,a.hero,dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.loose)moveToward(a.hero,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.15,z:clamp(a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.15,-19.3,PITCH.far-1)},attributes(developmentXP()).speed*fatigueFactor(match.energy)*1.15,dt);
 else if(a.runTarget&&(a.phase==='offball'||a.flying&&a.shotBy!=='return'))moveToward(a.hero,a.runTarget,4.5*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.combination&&['windup','flight'].includes(a.phase)&&a.shotBy==='return')moveToward(a.hero,a.returnSpot,4.6*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.passRecipient===a.hero&&a.shotBy==='teamPass'&&a.phase==='flight')moveToward(a.hero,{x:a.ball.x,z:a.ball.z},2.2*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.shotBy==='hero'&&a.followTarget)moveToward(a.hero,a.followTarget,1.35,dt);
 else if(a.phase!=='dribble')a.hero.speed=0;
}
function resolveBodyContacts(a){if(a.phase!=='windup'&&a.hero.y>0)a.hero.y=Math.max(0,a.hero.y-.02);const team=a.teammates||[];for(let i=0;i<team.length;i++){separateBodies(team[i],a.hero,.82,true);for(let j=i+1;j<team.length;j++)separateBodies(team[i],team[j],.82);}for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){for(const d of a.defenders){separateBodies(d,a.hero,.82,true);for(const t of a.teammates||[a.receiver])if(t)separateBodies(d,t,.82,true);}for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<a.defenders.length;j++)separateBodies(a.defenders[i],a.defenders[j],.82);}}
function tick(dt){if(!action||guideOpen)return;const a=action;if(a.phase==='interval'){tickInterval(dt);return;}if(a.phase==='card'){tickCard(a,dt);return;}if(a.phase==='incoming'){a.incomingTime+=dt;a.incomingPlayer.kickTimer+=dt;if(a.incomingTime>=.32){if(!a.incomingReleased){a.ball=launch(a.ball.vx===0&&a.ball.x!==undefined&&a.incomingMode?a.ball:a.incomingPlayer,a.incomingTarget,a.incomingMode||'low');a.incomingReleased=true;a.kicker=a.incomingPlayer;a.shotBy='teamPass';a.passRecipient=a.hero;
  // Lot 1 · A ground pass is a real pass: received, cut out, left loose or out, like any other ball.
  if(a.incomingMode!=='lob'){handOffIncoming();return;}}a.sim+=dt;a.flying=true;updateActors(a,dt);stepBall(a.ball,dt);if(a.incomingMode==='lob'&&a.incomingTarget.z<-15.5&&Math.abs(a.incomingTarget.x)<5.5){const k=a.keeper,tk=Math.hypot(k.x-a.incomingTarget.x,k.z-a.incomingTarget.z),th=Math.hypot(a.hero.x-a.incomingTarget.x,a.hero.z-a.incomingTarget.z);if(tk<th-.6&&a.ball.y<2.6&&a.ball.vy<0&&Math.hypot(a.ball.x-k.x,a.ball.z-k.z)<1.6){resolve(false,'Le gardien sort et capte le centre.',a.shotBy,'save');a.ballControl='held';k.holding=true;return;}}
  const reach=Math.hypot(a.ball.x-a.hero.x,a.ball.z-a.hero.z);
  if(a.incomingMode==='lob'&&reach<1.2&&a.ball.y>.75&&a.ball.y<2.6&&a.ball.vy<0){if(!aerialDuel())beginAerial();return;}
  if(a.ball.y<.75&&hitPlayer(a.ball,a.hero,.65,1.5)){beginAerial();return;}
  for(const d of a.defenders)if(a.ball.vy<0&&!d.crossTried&&hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.42,2.2)&&Math.hypot(a.ball.x-d.x,a.ball.z-d.z)<reach-.5){d.crossTried=true;if(Math.random()<.35){clearCross(d);return;}}
  // Once the cross has bounced it is an ordinary ball: no more automatic control.
  if(a.ball.bounces>0||a.ball.age>4)handOffIncoming();}return;}if(a.callPause){a.callWait=(a.callWait||0)+dt;if(a.callWait>=3){a.callPause=false;a.callWait=0;}}
 // V19 · Freeze frame: during a decision every player keeps his posture and momentum, like a paused video.
 if(!a.ready)a.runsPlanned=false;else if(!a.runsPlanned){a.runsPlanned=true;planRuns(a);}
 if((a.ready||a.callPause||a.phase==='choice'&&match.training||!a.started)&&!a.ended){a.frozen=true;return;}a.frozen=false;a.sim+=dt;drainEnergy((a.hero.speed||0)*dt*.09);updateActors(a,dt);
 if(!match.training){const k=a.keeper;if(k.state==='set'&&!a.flying&&!k.holding){const h=a.hero,dist=Math.hypot(h.x,h.z+20),alone=!a.defenders.some(d=>d.z<h.z&&Math.abs(d.x-h.x)<2.5&&d.z>-20);const want=-19.25+(alone&&dist<13?clamp((13-dist)/6,0,1)*1.9:0);k.rush=false;if(alone&&dist<17&&['dribble','skill'].includes(a.phase))keeperRush(k,dt);else k.z+=clamp(want-k.z,-2.5*dt,2.5*dt);}updateKeeper(k,a.ball,dt,a.flying&&a.shotBy!=='return');if(!a.ended&&k.rush&&keeperSmother(k))return;}
 if(a.hero.grounded&&a.sim>a.hero.grounded){a.hero.grounded=0;a.hero.bicycle=false;}
 if(!match.training)a.offsideLine=offsideLineZ(a);
 if(a.ready){attachBall(a.hero);}
 else if(a.phase==='dribble'){a.dribbleElapsed+=dt;const held=a.defenders.find(d=>d.shoulder&&Math.hypot(a.hero.x-d.x,a.hero.z-d.z)<1.1);a.hero.lean=held?-held.lean:0;/* Lot 4 · the carrier runs flat out through his touch (no braking before the end of it), as fast as a defender of the same level */const reached=moveToward(a.hero,a.dribbleEnd,attributes(developmentXP()).speed*fatigueFactor(match.energy)*(held?.86:1),dt,14,false);attachBall(a.hero,true);
  if(held&&Math.random()<dt*.5*match.profile.press*(held.style==='engagé'?1.3:1)){const b=a.ball;b.vx=(a.hero.vx||0)*.7+(a.hero.x-held.x)*2;b.vz=(a.hero.vz||0)*.7+(a.hero.z-held.z)*2;a.kicker=a.hero;makeLoose('Duel d’épaule perdu : le ballon t’échappe !',a.hero);}
  else for(const d of a.defenders){if(slideContact(d))break;if(d.challenge?.kind==='slide')continue;const close=Math.hypot(a.hero.x-d.x,a.hero.z-d.z);if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)||Math.hypot(a.ball.x-d.x,a.ball.z-d.z)<.59||close<.95&&!d.shoulder&&Math.random()<dt*1.5*match.profile.press){winBall(d,'Ballon récupéré par le défenseur. Essaie de le contourner.');break;}}if(!a.ended&&(reached||a.dribbleElapsed>1.7))controlAgain('Dribble terminé · pause. Choisis la prochaine touche ou prépare ta frappe.');updateDistance();}
 else if(a.phase==='skill'){
  const k=a.skill;k.time+=dt;a.hero.skillTimer=k.time;const t=Math.min(1,k.time/k.duration),smooth=t*t*(3-2*t);a.hero.x=k.start.x+(k.end.x-k.start.x)*smooth;a.hero.z=k.start.z+(k.end.z-k.start.z)*smooth;a.hero.speed=2.5;
  a.ball={x:k.ballStart.x+(k.end.x-k.ballStart.x)*smooth,y:R,z:k.ballStart.z+(k.end.z-.58-k.ballStart.z)*smooth,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0};
  for(const d of a.defenders){if(slideContact(d))break;if(d.challenge?.kind==='slide')continue;if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)||hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.35,.8)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur a lu le râteau. Essaie l’autre côté ou un autre timing.');break;}}
  // A completed rake wrong-foots the defenders who were close: they need a moment to turn.
  if(!a.ended&&t>=1){for(const d of a.defenders)if(Math.hypot(d.x-a.hero.x,d.z-a.hero.z)<3.2)d.stun=Math.max(d.stun||0,.45);controlAgain('Râteau terminé · pause. À toi de choisir la suite.');}updateDistance();
 }
 else if(a.phase==='press'){tickPress(a,dt);}
 else if(a.phase==='shield'){a.shieldTime+=dt;const d=a.defenders[a.pressIndex];if(d){a.hero.angle=Math.atan2(-(a.hero.x-d.x),-(a.hero.z-d.z));if(Math.hypot(d.x-a.hero.x,d.z-a.hero.z)<1&&Math.random()<dt*.35){winBall(d,'Le défenseur passe devant et récupère le ballon.');return;}}a.hero.speed=0;attachBall(a.hero);if(a.shieldTime>=1.3)controlAgain('Ballon protégé. Ton partenaire est venu en soutien.');}
 else if(a.phase==='feint'){a.feintTime+=dt;a.hero.speed=0;attachBall(a.hero);if(a.feintTime>=.42)controlAgain($('feedback').textContent);}
 else if(a.phase==='windup'){a.windup.elapsed+=dt;if(a.aerial){const ae=a.aerial;if(ae.bicycle){a.hero.y=ae.jump+.55*Math.sin(Math.min(1,a.windup.elapsed/.3)*Math.PI/2);if(a.windup.elapsed>=.3)releaseBall();}else{if(ae.header)a.hero.y=ae.jump;if(a.windup.elapsed>=.12)releaseBall();}}else{attachBall(a.windup.player);if(a.windup.elapsed>=.28)releaseBall();}}
 else if(a.phase==='choice'){attachBall(a.receiver);a.decisionTime=(a.decisionTime||0)+dt;if(a.decisionTime>.4)playTeamDecision(teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.runTarget?a.hero:null));}
 else if(a.phase==='offball'){a.runTime+=dt;attachBall(a.receiver);for(const d of a.defenders){if(hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.35,1.4)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur a coupé la remise.');break;}}if(!a.ended&&(Math.hypot(a.hero.x-a.runTarget.x,a.hero.z-a.runTarget.z)<.8||a.runTime>1.1)){a.returnSpot={...a.runTarget};const pick=teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.hero);/* Lot 1 · The call makes the run more attractive; the partner still weighs every option. */if(pick.kind==='pass'&&pick.receiver===a.hero&&laneClearance(a.receiver,a.returnSpot,a.defenders)>.85){windUp(a.receiver,a.returnSpot,'low','return');}else{a.runTarget=null;a.combination=false;playTeamDecision(pick);if(pick.kind!=='shot')$('feedback').textContent=pick.kind==='carry'?'Ton partenaire a vu ton appel, mais il garde le ballon.':'Ton partenaire a vu ton appel, mais il choisit une autre solution.';}}}
 else if(a.phase==='teammateRun'){a.carryTime=(a.carryTime||0)+dt;const arrived=moveToward(a.receiver,a.teamTarget,3.4,dt);attachBall(a.receiver,true);for(const d of a.defenders)if(hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.38,1.5)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur récupère le ballon.');break;}if(!a.ended&&(arrived||a.carryTime>1.1))playTeamDecision(teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.runTarget?a.hero:null));}
 else if(a.flying){a.flightTime+=dt;const before=stepBall(a.ball,dt);
  const contacts=[];for(const p of [...a.defenders,...a.teammates,a.hero]){if(p===a.kicker&&!a.loose||p===a.contactIgnore&&a.sim<a.ignoreUntil)continue;/* Lot 3 · the pass that opens the chance always reaches the player */if(a.incomingPass&&p!==a.hero)continue;const opponent=a.defenders.includes(p);const contact=sweptPlayerContact(before,a.ball,p,opponent?.38:.65,(opponent?1.7:1.55)+(p.y||0));if(contact)contacts.push({p,opponent,contact});}
  contacts.sort((x,y)=>x.contact.t-y.contact.t);const touch=contacts[0];
  if(touch){const {p,opponent,contact}=touch;Object.assign(a.ball,{x:contact.x,y:contact.y,z:contact.z});if(!opponent&&isOffsidePlayer(p)){callOffside(p);return;}if(opponent){if(challengeHits(p,a.ball)&&p.challenge?.kind==='block')deflectBall(p);else winBall(p,'Intercepté ! Le défenseur a fermé la trajectoire.');}else if(p===a.hero){if(a.incomingPass&&a.ball.y<.75){a.incomingPass=false;beginAerial();$('feedback').textContent='Bien reçu · contrôle orienté ou joue directement.';return;}controlAgain('Bien reçu · à toi de rejouer !');return;}else{a.receiver=p;receivePass();return;}}

  const frame=!a.ended&&frameContact(before,a.ball);if(frame){makeLoose(frame==='bar'?'Barre ! Suis le second ballon.':'Poteau ! Suis le second ballon.');say('frame');}
  if(!a.ended&&!(a.contactIgnore===a.keeper&&a.sim<a.ignoreUntil)){const contact=sweptKeeperTouch(before,a.ball,a.keeper);if(contact){Object.assign(a.ball,contact);/* Lot 4 · into his body he holds it; at full stretch only a soft ball is held, the rest is pushed away */if(Math.hypot(a.ball.vx,a.ball.vz)>(Math.abs(a.ball.x-a.keeper.x)<.55?25:17)){a.ball.vx=(a.ball.x-a.keeper.x)*4;a.ball.vz=Math.max(4,Math.abs(a.ball.vz)*.28);a.ball.vy=2;a.ball.z=a.keeper.z+.85;makeLoose('Le gardien repousse ! Le ballon est encore jouable.',a.keeper);}else resolve(false,'Arrêt du gardien ! Le ballon est bloqué dans ses mains.',a.shotBy,'save');}}
  if(!a.ended&&a.loose&&a.ball.y<1.5&&hitPlayer(a.ball,a.hero,.65,1.3)){controlAgain('Second ballon récupéré · pause. À toi de rejouer !');return;}
  if(!a.ended&&!a.loose&&Math.hypot(a.ball.vx,a.ball.vz)<.7&&a.flightTime>2)makeLoose('Ballon libre : tente de le récupérer.');
  const crossing=goalCrossing(before,a.ball);if(!a.ended&&!frame&&crossing){if(crossing.inside)resolve(true,a.shotBy==='teammate'?(a.goalAssist?'BUUUT ! Passe décisive pour toi.':'BUUUT ! Le mouvement collectif est récompensé.'):a.combination?'BUUUT ! Tu conclus le une-deux.':'BUUUT ! Le ballon finit dans les filets.');else resolve(false,crossing.y>2.44?'Au-dessus ! La trajectoire dépasse la barre.':'À côté ! Le ballon passe hors du cadre.');}
  if(!a.ended&&(Math.abs(a.ball.x)>PITCH.W+.5||a.ball.z>PITCH.far||a.ball.age>(a.loose?7:5)||a.ball.z<-24))resolve(false,a.shotBy==='return'?'La remise est interceptée ou trop longue.':'Occasion manquée. Ajuste la direction et la puissance.');
 }else if(a.ended){
  a.after+=dt;
  if(a.ballControl==='net')stepNetBall(a.ball,dt);
  else if(a.ballControl==='held')Object.assign(a.ball,keeperGrip(a.keeper),{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0});
  else if(a.ballControl==='defender')attachBall(a.newOwner,a.after<1.1);
  else if(a.ballControl==='loose'&&a.after<4)stepBall(a.ball,dt);
  if(a.after>.9){if(!match.training)$('continue').hidden=false;if(!replay&&a.tape?.length>=TAPE_HZ)$('replayBtn').hidden=false;}
 }
 resolveBodyContacts(a);
}
function loop(t){const dt=Math.min((t-lastTime)/1000,.04);lastTime=t;time+=dt;if(action&&!document.hidden&&replay)playReplay(dt);else if(action&&!document.hidden){if(drag&&(drag.kind==='ball'||drag.kind==='target'&&Date.now()-drag.began>=LOB_HOLD_MS))updateAim(drag.current);let remaining=dt;while(remaining>0){const d=Math.min(remaining,1/120);tick(d);remaining-=d;}if(!guideOpen)recordFrame(action,dt);if(action){if(action.phase==='interval')drawTactical($('tacticalPitch'),match.tactical);else engine.render(action,dt,time);}updateOffscreen();updateCoach();updateCrowd();updateReplayTimer();if(match?.commentAt!=null&&time-match.commentAt>3.4&&!$('commentary').hidden)$('commentary').hidden=true;engine.setRunCursor?.(action&&action.runTarget&&!action.ended&&action.phase!=='interval'?action.runTarget:null,!!action?.callPause);if(action?.ended&&!match?.training&&action.after>continueDelay(action)&&!action.autoNext){action.autoNext=true;$('continue').onclick();}}frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}
async function init(){$('fullscreen').hidden=!$('match').requestFullscreen;refresh();setMode('normal');try{const {Pitch3D}=await import('./engine.js?v=20-9k');engine=new Pitch3D(canvas,career);engine.setCrowdVisible($('crowdToggle').checked);(async()=>{try{const m=await import('./mh.js?v=20-9k');engine.mh=await m.loadMH('./mh/head.json?v=mh1');}catch(err){console.warn('tête MakeHuman indisponible',err);}})();$('engineStatus').textContent='Prêt à entrer sur le terrain · '+BUILD;if($('footBuild'))$('footBuild').textContent=BUILD+' · Entre sur le terrain';$('play').disabled=false;$('practice').disabled=false;frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}catch(e){console.error(e);$('engineStatus').textContent='La 3D est indisponible. Essaie Safari ou Chrome avec WebGL activé.';$('play').disabled=true;}}
canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(action){action=null;match=null;screen('home');}$('play').disabled=true;$('engineStatus').textContent='Affichage 3D interrompu. Recharge la page pour reprendre.';});init();
